**Checks of `test_goldens_collision.py` (sections 12 and 13, fourteen new):** the tone harmony reaches the woven lens with the texture only (a trio, a family, a chain, a Kiss pair, a wide pupil pair, the stack lens and the straight band are byte for byte the same at full strength and off); on a woven pair it changes the picture and nothing of the plan; it changes nothing outside the band (every differing pixel lies inside both discs with a colour weight strictly between 0 and 1); it fades out toward the edge of the band; it is toward the lighter iris and within the share (no pixel lifted by more than 0.30 of the largest gap); the fibres keep their contrast and colour (the pull adds the same lightness to a pixel whatever its own value); the share is a strength; the budgets and the self check hold with it; the coarse tone field is a function of the irises and the geometry alone; and for the tongues: the same reach (woven lens with the texture only), another colour field and the same line, fibre switch and tone field, a contour at most 0.8 of the phase 2 length, nothing outside the seam changes, the budgets and the self check hold alone and with the tone harmony. v3coll 129 to 143. `PAIR_FINISH` and `PROTOTYPE_FINISH` of `collision_cases.py` have the two options (off), so the replay of step A and every golden are the old pictures.

**Turned on (the second commit of the phase, `ENGINE_V` 8).** `PAIR_FINISH` is now `band_tone` 0.30 and `tongue` "round" (`collision_cases.py` the same, the goldens are recorded with it). The strength was chosen by looking at 0, 0.15, 0.30 and 1.0, with the profile sin^2(pi w) and, in the laboratory, with 4 w (1 - w) and with a profile that peaks at w 0.8: 0.30 is the share the owner approved, the profile with no slope at the edges of the band is the one that cannot make a kink in the crossfade, and since no strength can be told apart at the size of the picture, the choice is the one that cannot do harm (the largest change of a pixel is 5 to 13 levels). A chroma pull (the laboratory pulled the chroma of the more chromatic iris toward the other's by 30 percent inside the band, with the profile 4 w (1 - w)) lowers the step inside the band by a further 3 to 5 percent on bb, bbs and gb2 (23.7 to 22.5, 22.7 to 21.9, 16.9 to 16.0) and moves nothing across the seam (33.90 to 33.90); it was not built: it is as small (the step across the seam does not move, 33.90 to 33.90, and the pull of a chroma gap of 23 units is 30 percent of it weighted by the iris's share of the pixel), the instruction was to ship lightness alone when a lightness pull alone helps and chroma does not, and here neither shows. The profile of the colours across the seam (DG1's sampling, `evidence\seam_profile_refs_vs_ours_L_C.json`) says what the references have in their band: H11's seam centre is 4.2 units of L* darker than the mean of its two sides and its chroma there is 6.6, H10's is 2.9 lighter with a chroma of 21.7; ours lies between 4.9 darker and 2.0 lighter with a chroma of 5.3 to 27.1 (bb 12.4, bbs 11.5): the band of ours is neither systematically lighter nor greyer than the references', so the references give no tone target for the band.

**What moved.** Pictures: the ten woven masters (bb, bbs, br3, gb2, vd in Clean Infinity and in Collision Infinity, 4096 px); the other 17 of the 27 round 1 masters (br2, gb, gbs and vd2 in the two infinity styles, which fall back to the Kiss geometry or the stack lens, and the nine Kiss masters) are pixel identical to origin/main with both switches on (decoded pixels). Goldens (`scripts/styles_tests/data`, recorded again by `record_goldens_collision_repo.py --designs infinity` and `--real` with `SNAPEYES_CALIB` the DG1 fixtures, reviewed against the old files by record): `collision_goldens.json` 18 of 62, every infinity picture that draws the weave (blue with brown at 512, 1024, 4096 and with the words, blue with green at 512 on five canvases and with the brief edge and at 1024, the dark brown pair, grey with amber at 1024 and Clean Infinity at 1:1 with and without the words, the three Clean Infinity pictures of blue with brown and blue with green), in 16 of the 18 the visible share of the irises (`vis`) moved with the picture, in 2 only the picture; `collision_goldens_real.json` 16 of 21 (the eight board pairs in both builds); the stack lens, the hard seam, the wide pupil pair (the Kiss geometry), every Kiss, trio, family and chain record are the old hashes, and every other field of every record, its seed and its frozen choices are the old ones. `engine_v.json` records version 8 and the two new hashes. The same 18 and 16 as in phase 2, for the same reason: the picture of a woven lens is what the two switches reach.

**Budgets with both on** (the masters, `SP\wave-tone\final\evidence\metrics_final_matrix_4096.json`): the mixed share 1.98 to 2.32 percent (before 1.99 to 2.32), the support 2.08 to 2.47 (2.09 to 2.43), the seam band of T1 2.19 to 2.61 of the limit 4 (2.19 to 2.57); the tone harmony adds nothing to any of them (the plan is the same plan), the rounder tongues move them by at most 0.09 (gb2 in Clean Infinity, support 2.38 to 2.47), inside the limits of reading A (3 and 4) that stay as they are. **Robustness**: every ordered pair of the 23 gate-passing calibration eyes at 512 px with the self check: Clean Infinity 506 pairs (441 woven, 65 that fall back to the Kiss geometry), Collision Infinity 169 pairs, every third (147 woven, 22 that fall back): no exception, no non-finite pixel, every check passes (T1, T2, T3 with its floors, T6, T19, the seam budgets), the largest mixed share 2.41 and 2.34 percent, support 2.55 and 2.47, seam band 2.75 and 2.65 (phase 2: 2.47, 2.62, 2.79 and 2.39, 2.54, 2.67).

**Suites** (the private fixtures, two suites at a time because the machine is shared, scratch on drive D). The first commit alone: v3coll 143 (was 129), v3uni 142, v3compose 125, v3single 118, v3steps 152, v3checkout 139, v3core 86, every count at the baseline, and `npm run build` exit 0. The full set on the final tree, one run of 39 minutes: **40 of 40 green**, every count equal to `suites/baseline.json` (v3coll 143, ts 562, the rest as recorded; `oldpay` and `fixer` passed in this run), and `npm run build` exit 0 on that tree. v3coll with the real calibration eyes (`SNAPEYES_CALIB` the DG1 fixtures): 143 of 143 and seven LOCAL lines ok, among them the 21 real-eye pictures equal the recording byte for byte, step A on the real eyes, resolve against the render on 14 real pairs, and the owner's budgets with every check of the self check on the real pairs. `python suites/compile_matrix.py`: compileall api ok under Python 3.12, 3.13 and 3.14, 100 files parse as 3.12. The robustness sweep over every ordered pair of the calibration eyes and the pictures of the ten woven masters are in `SP\wave-toneinal\evidence`.

**Not measured, not done:** a blind panel against H10 and H11 (the looks above are one reviewer's), the lens width (the geometry of `solve_infinity_d` is the owner's, the cause is the pupils), a tone or chroma match of the lens outside the band (it breaks the integrity rule T1: the owner's decision, with the arithmetic above as the ceiling).

## 42. The visitor counter and admin v2, phase A (2026-10-20): a cookie-free counter, the campaign code, five visitor pages, the feedback box

(Sections 40 and 41 belong to other waves.) Five commits on origin/main a498855, local only (no push, no deployment, no model call): the counter, the campaign code, the visitor pages, the feedback box, and this section with the tests and the numbers.

**The counter** (`api/_lib/counter.py`, `events.py` kind `hit`, switch `SNAPEYES_COUNTER`, on by default). One tiny beacon per step of a visit (landing, try, photo, result, soon, buy, order) on the price test's beacon route (`POST /api/checkout {hit, camp, lang, market}`: no new function). The server keeps codes only: step, campaign code, language, market, device class, browser family (the Facebook, Instagram and TikTok in-app browsers are told apart), country (two letters), and per day the counts in the `hits`, `hit_cc`, `hit_ms`, `fx` and `fx_ms` tables with caps on their keys. No cookie, no visitor number, no IP address, no user agent, no URL. The landing's beacon is an inline script in the head of `index.html` (334 B gzip in the HTML, 0 B in the assets: the landing's JavaScript does not grow); `/try` and `/order` use `src/shared/counter.ts` (361 B in their shared chunk).

**The campaign code** travels from the link (`utm_source`, `utm_medium`, `utm_campaign`, `utm_term`, `utm_content`, or the kind of page the visitor came from) through `sessionStorage["snapeyes.c"]` into the beacons, every request of the tools (the events of analyze, enhance, compose, checkout get the code and the classes), the order record (`checkout.camp`, `paid.json camp`) and Stripe's metadata `src`. `fbclid` is never read.

**The visitor pages** of `/admin` (Lithuanian): Saltiniai, Piltuvas, Kodel nepavyksta, Nuotrauku kokybe, Reklamos (the owner's spend ledger with Meta's 21 percent VAT, the cost per step, ROAS and the traffic lights of the ads plan), Atsiliepimai, the source column and filter of the orders and a daily block of the summary. `scripts/seed_demo_traffic.py` fills a local store with a seeded month so every page can be looked at (the screenshots in `SP/wave-admin/final/owner/`).

**The feedback box** (`api/_lib/feedback.py`, `src/try/Feedback.tsx`, switch `SNAPEYES_FEEDBACK`): six chips and an optional text of up to 300 characters on the result screen and on the retake card of /try only, four languages, no e-mail field; the answer is an event of codes (12 months), the text a separate row kept 90 days, filtered (no @, mail host, telephone number, link, markup, noise), rate limited, owner can delete.

**Privacy text** (en, de, lt, hu; the Australian edition by `patchDoc`, the Hungarian edition): the paragraphs of the operations section, the storage section, the order and retention rows, the legal basis. NOT reviewed by counsel or by native readers of Lithuanian and Hungarian (said in the commits; `SP/wave-admin/final/COUNSEL_PARAGRAPH.md`). `LEGAL_UPDATED` 2026-10-20.

**New suites.** `v3counter` 78 (in the world before the cutover), `v3traffic` 53 (the pages over an empty store, a seeded fortnight, the filters, orders and revenue tied to a campaign, the ledger and its refusals, over HTTP every report keeps its keys, a seeded month holds codes only), `v3feedback` 54 (the filter, the answer over HTTP, the kill switch, the counts, retention, no personal data after hostile requests, the admin actions). `ts` 562 to 739 (the counter's page tests, `admin_traffic`, `feedback_client`, `feedback_ui`, `legal_counter`, `legal_feedback`). **Pins that moved on purpose**, each with its reason in the file, none lowered: `exp` (the reply of GET /api/checkout gains `counter` and `feedback`), `v3admin` (the event kinds gain `hit` and `fb`), `au` (`eu_counter.json` regenerated; B2b now names the counter's and the feedback box's paragraphs: operations is 5 blocks added and 2 removed), `v3checkout`, `v3cutover` and `catalogue_page` (the legal date), `legal_counter` (eight paragraphs in operations).

**Bytes.** `check:budget` (the gate counts what the landing can request): the counter's, the legal text's and the visitor pages' growth is recorded as allowances (`COUNTER` 391 B JS, `LEGAL_TEXT` 8,074 B JS in the legal pages' entry chunk, `ADMIN_VIEWS` 56 B CSS and `FEEDBACK` 131 B CSS in the Tailwind stylesheet the tools and the panel share), each measured on its commit minus its parent; what is left is +7,767 B JavaScript and +7,176 B CSS over the motionless page (section 39: +7,852 and +7,179), under the hard stops as before.

**Lessons.** A report must not use the key `ms` (`iris.run` replaces it with the call's own time: found by looking at the page, now checked over HTTP). The route's origin gate also reads `Referer`. A headless browser is a robot to the counter (give a test browser an ordinary user agent). On Windows with `core.autocrlf` a script that inserts a line next to a CRLF line must not leave a lone CR: git then stops normalising the whole file.

**Full set**: the private fixtures (`SNAPEYES_FIXTURES`), two suites at a time, scratch on drive D (`SNAPEYES_SUITES_OUT`), `npm run build` first (tsc, check:prices, check:texts, check:styles: exit 0), `npm run lint` (exit 0: 53 warnings, the 43 of origin/main and 10 more of the same two kinds, fast refresh and setState in an effect, in the new admin files) and the Python compile matrix (3.12, 3.13, 3.14: ok): one run on the final tree, 47 min 50 s, **43 of 43 green**, every count equal to `suites/baseline.json` (`ts` 739, `v3counter` 78, `v3traffic` 53, `v3feedback` 54; `oldpay` passed, no rerun). The browser checks, each against the final build, all exit 0: `check:shell`, `check:landing` (the same 2 notes as origin/main; phone slow 4G LCP 1,020 to 1,256 ms against the 1.6 s target, CLS 0.0004, so the inline beacon costs the first screen nothing), `check:motion`, `check:motion:sections`, `check:motion:phone` (all ok), `check:motion:close`, `check:reveal`, `check:assets`, `check:slots`, and `check:budget` (+7,767 B JavaScript and +7,176 B CSS over the motionless page).

**Not measured.** Real traffic (every number in the screenshots is seeded). WebKit and a real iPhone for the radio chips and the keyboard, a screen reader pass of the box, and the in-app browsers' real user agents beyond the patterns the tests use. Phase B (an opt-in to share a photo) does not exist and is OFF; the daily e-mail summary and alerts (Resend) and cohorts (who comes back) are not built.

## 43. QA follow-up fixes (2026-10-20): the share preview per language, the sample eye, the language switch in the flow, the polish

Branch `v3-qa2` on origin/main 31156d3 (the go-live page test: ordering closed), four commits, each with a check that fails on the old code. No price, no legal text, no watermark wording, no engine rule and no paid chain was touched; no model call but the one of the sample re-bake (below). Local only: nothing was pushed or deployed.

**1. The share preview in the visitor's language** (`02cc5a1`). The build writes `dist/lp/en.html`, `de.html`, `lt.html`, `hu.html` (each its own `<html lang>`, title, description, canonical, og:url, og:locale and alternates, og:title, og:description, og:image and alt, twitter tags, from `src/landing/copy/<lang>.json` meta, `src/shared/lang.ts` and the new `src/landing/shell/share.json`), and `vercel.json` rewrites `/` by `?lang=` (then `?m=lt`, `?m=hu`, else English) to `/lp/<lang>`. There is **no `dist/index.html`**: Vercel checks the file system before the rewrites (proven on the real Vercel CLI 64.7.0's own build output, `vercel build` run locally with no login: the routes of the rewrites come after `{"handle": "filesystem"}`), so a file for `/` would answer by itself and no rewrite for `/` could fire; a destination is the clean path `/lp/de`, never `.html` (the cleanUrls redirect would catch it with a 308). Three share pictures `og-founder-eye-de.jpg`, `-lt.jpg`, `-hu.jpg` (1200 x 630, 89,329 / 89,917 / 87,298 bytes, under 150 kB), drawn by `scripts/make_og_cards.py` from the English card (its eye pixels; the words in the site's fonts at sizes measured on the English card, `--parity` redraws the English card with a mean grey difference of 0.60 in the text areas); the headline and the second line are the page's own hero title and first micro line of the language, only the caption and the alt texts are new words. `/try` has its share tags (English, one file: a file for `/try` would shadow a rewrite for it the same way; its script follows the language). **`scripts/check_langheads.mjs`** (`npm run check:langheads`): the build read as a crawler (`facebookexternalhit`, no script) through `scripts/lib/static.mjs`, which now answers like Vercel (file system first, then the rewrites with their `has` conditions, a `.html` destination caught by the 308): 16 links, four editions, every head tag against the copy, one canonical, identical bodies, cards, `/try`, the template; with `--browser` in real Chrome (the page boots, the head survives the script, a language switch updates title, canonical and og:url). The old one-file build fails it with 218 problems (the QA's findings, one by one). The checks that read `dist/index.html` read `dist/lp/en.html` (`check_landing`, `check_landing_states`, `check_shell`). `check_texts` lints `share.json`.

**2. The sample eye** (`647a828`). The shipped `sample_eye_blue_restored.jpg` passed the lid rule and failed the fill rule (`fill_hard_edges`, n_step 127 of 180 against the limit of 120), which held the Universe tile and raised the retake card for a picture the visitor did not take. Fixed with a better sample, not an exemption: `scripts/bake_sample.py` runs the stored client crop through the current chain (deglare, then the first restoration of `api/enhance.py` `_draw`, ONE image-model call, about 0.07 USD of the 0.30 cap, the second restoration forced off; the key through `SNAPEYES_DEV_KEYFILE=1`, never printed). The one draw passes both rules (lid 0 / 0 / 10 outliers / 15.1; fill run18 2, n_step 95, catch 0; severity 0.0; fidelity 0.917) and replaces the file (269,375 bytes, was 294,739); `SAMPLE_SHA256` is `4c4429b2...`. The gate, its thresholds and the sample route are unchanged. Proof: v3gate +4, v3compose +1, ts +4 (`suites/ts/sample_eye.test.ts`: the REAL tile answer of the engine for the new and the old file), and end to end in real Chrome against the real engine code (`scripts/dev_api.py`, no model: `{sample: true}` makes none): with the new file `/api/enhance` tells the page both rules ok, `/api/compose` lists every tile available, no retake card; with the old file Universe is `gate` and the card says "Your eye needs a retake".

**3. The language switch in the middle of the flow** (`1b7fcf8`). `/api/analyze` writes its sentences in all four languages in one analysis (`quality.words`; the fields of the request's language are exactly as before), `src/try/shots.ts` `wordsOf` reads the language the page speaks now, and `/api/enhance {redisplay: true, sealed, lang[, crop, pad]}` draws the display copy of an eye already restored again in the new language (no model call, no ticket, only the sealed KIND_ORDER preview of this server is accepted; the Reveal's copy when the deglared crop comes with it); `Eye.wm` is the language of an eye's watermark and `TryApp` asks once per switch. New suite **v3lang** (18), `suites/ts/lang_switch.test.ts` (11) and in real Chrome `scripts/check_lang_switch.mjs` (`npm run check:langswitch`, 60 checks: four languages as the start, in the quality step, during the analysis, after and during the restoration); the old client fails it in every combination.

**4. The polish** (`3adf3f7`). `favicon.svg` is the iris ring of the header (it was the default Vite logo), `favicon.ico`, the touch icon, the app icons and `manifest.webmanifest`; a branded 404 (`src/notfound`, written into `dist/404.html`, four languages, status 404 by Vercel); the language buttons, logos and the quiet link of the landing and `/try` answer a touch over 44 px of height (a pseudo-element: no box moves; rows of links 8 to 10 px apart take the 24 px between their neighbours; phone-width language buttons stay 32 px wide); "Take a photo" on the first screen of `/try` on a screen under 820 px tall (the guide comes after the buttons); the HEIC sentence says what to do. `scripts/check_polish.mjs` (`npm run check:polish`, 72 checks). No CSP and no Permissions-Policy.

**Bytes** (`check:budget`, gzip level 6, against the page without motion). Landing JavaScript +7,799 B (hard stop +8,000; +7,823 on origin/main), landing CSS +7,297 B (hard stop +7,500; +7,192 on origin/main: +105 B, the hit areas of the landing's language buttons, logos, quiet link and footer links; 203 B left), `/try` JavaScript 65,100 to 65,824 B gzip (+724 B for the items 1, 3 and 4 together) and the CSS the two tools share 2,284 to 2,305 B (+21 B: the hit-area rule), both outside the landing's budget but inside `check_motion_flow`'s (+2,048 B of CSS over the page before the motion: the first version of the hit areas, written as Tailwind utilities, was 3 B over it and became one small rule in `src/motion/flow.css`). Pages: `lp/*.html` 33.7 to 34.0 kB each (8.3 kB gzip) against the one `index.html` of 32.7 kB; `404.html` 5.3 kB; share cards 87 to 90 kB; sample 269,375 B (was 294,739); icons: `favicon.svg` 324 B (was 9,522), `favicon.ico` 8,287, `apple-touch-icon.png` 17,881, `icon-192.png` 19,140, `icon-512.png` 52,257 (fetched only when asked for).

**Full set**: the private fixtures, two suites at a time (the runner's `xargs -P 3` set to 2 for this run, not committed), scratch on drive D: **41 of 41 green** (40 suites and `ts`), every count equal to `suites/baseline.json` (`v3lang` 18 new, `ts` 577, `v3gate` 105, `v3compose` 126), about 40 minutes. On the final build, all exit 0: `npm run build`, `npm run lint` (the same 43 warnings as on origin/main), `tsc -b`, `check:prices`, `check:texts`, `check:styles`, `check:assets`, `check:gate` (27 of 27), `check:slots`, `check:budget`, `check:shell`, `check:tryheader`, `check:langheads` with `--browser`, `check:langswitch`, `check:polish`, and in real Chrome `check:landing` (82 ok, 2 notes, twice: on the build before the last CSS change and on the final one), `check:states` (1,112 of 1,112), `check:motion:flow` and `check:legal` (identical to origin/main: 36 pages, pixels, every box and computed style).

**Not measured and risks.** The rewrite of `/` is the one thing that cannot be shown without a deployment: the routes Vercel computes from this `vercel.json` and the file-system-first order are proven on its own CLI's build output and the local server emulates them, but nothing here ran on Vercel's edge. After the first Preview deploy run `curl -s -A facebookexternalhit/1.1 "https://<preview>/?lang=de" | grep -E "og:(title|url|image|locale)"` for each language (and the Sharing Debugger once) before promoting; if `/` ever answered 404 the change reverts by restoring `dist/index.html` (take out the `langPages` plugin and the rewrites). The new words (the card captions and alt texts, the 404, the HEIC sentence) were written here and need a native read in German and Hungarian (Lithuanian by the owner).

## 44. The light family (2026-10-20): Supernova, Nebula, Sparks, Halo and Aurora, five styles of one eye at stage preview

The owner chose five of the day's laboratory looks ("taip, darom Nebula, Supernova, Halo, Sparks, Aurora"; not Obsidian, not Bezel, and the live Celestial Gold is left as it is). Three commits on `v3-int3` `e2754c1`, local only: no push, no deployment, no environment change, no model call.

**The family.** `api/_lib/styles/lumen` (`common`, `nebula`, `stardust`, `halo`, `sparks`, `aurora`) is an engine family of its own, not five more designs of `singles`: the singles family keeps its seven files and six registry styles, so `v3single` replays its goldens byte for byte (one check of it moved: the customer's list for one eye has eleven styles now). The family runs the singles pipeline (`kit`: the grade, the F3 feather, the effect on the float canvas, the tone map, the dither, the iris pasted LAST, the customer's words) with its own geometry table (`GEOM`) and implements the contract of the style package (`resolve`, `preview`, `tiles`), so `/api/compose` draws its tiles and 1024 px previews and the master step its 4096 px file with the self check, through the dispatcher and nothing else. No plate, no atlas, no model call. Nebula and Supernova read `ctx.discs` and `ctx.irises` as lists, so the pair and family styles can take `nebula.fx_nebula` and `stardust.fx_stardust` over unchanged (`v3lumen` section 7 draws a pair and a family of three with them, every iris byte for byte its graded disc). The design id of Supernova is `stardust`: the legacy id `supernova` is a word that only the registry may write (style check item 3). `ENGINE_FAMILIES` and the build's module list name `lumen`.

**The registry.** `solo.supernova`, `solo.nebula`, `solo.sparks`, `solo.halo`, `solo.aurora`: group solo, one eye, one layout, the art price class, no pick (the recommended tile is still the release 1 table), tile order 7 to 11 after the six of release 1, ceiling `preview` (as Radiance: the free preview, the buy card says soon, checkout answers 409 stage; the admin page cannot raise a ceiling, so making one orderable is a reviewed change of the literal and then the owner's L1, L0 and live). The slug `supernova` is shared with the retired legacy id of the same name (item 2 allows it for one name), so the tile images `style-supernova-*.webp` show the new look under the old file name, as the cutover did for `celestial-gold`. **Gate: advisory with the lid rule, as Radiance's.** The looks draw light round the limb and nothing over the iris, and the self checks (T1, T2, T3, T6, T7, T12, T18) pass on every eye of the suites and the boards, so nothing argues for hard; Halo is the closest to Clean Iris (the owner made Clean Iris hard after a delivered file kept lid skin at the rim), so it is the one to make hard if he wants every plain-iris look to refuse a failing eye.

**What changed from the laboratory code.** Supernova's haze and Nebula's composition, dust and glow are drawn one band of grid rows at a time: the same looks, and a 4096 px master needs +458 to +546 MB where the laboratory needed +662 (Supernova) and +710 (Nebula), against Radiance's +819 to +822 and Clean Iris's +523 to +527 (the grade of the 4096 px eye is the floor of every single). A Nebula master draws two finer octaves of the gas and of the filaments (`common.fbm_master`: an octave is drawn where its cells span at least 8 px at the master's size and fewer at 1024), so its gas is clumpy and fibrous at 100 percent instead of a smooth blur, and the 1024 px preview is the same picture (a master reduced to 1024 differs from the preview by 3.5 of 255 on average, 2.4 without the octaves). Aurora: the laboratory's veils ran to the canvas edge, so the picture read as a lit square with a frame; the crown now fades into a dark sky between 0.31 and 0.50 S from the iris, its tips are the eye's hue turned 90 degrees (it was 56) and its rays have more contrast (2.6, it was 2.1), and its veils are drawn on a grid of 1536 cells (2048): a two coloured crown of curtains, apart from Radiance's thin rays out of a dark moat. Halo and Sparks are the laboratory's looks.

**Numbers** (one render per fresh process through the master step's own call, `styles.preview` at 4096 px with the self check, the 4K restorations of a blue and a dark brown eye, Windows, one thread, the machine shared with two other builders; `SP\wave-singles5\final\timing_memory_4096.jsonl`):

| look | wall with the 4096 px grade | art step | memory | 1024 px preview, the design alone | 480 px tile |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Supernova | 11.8 to 14.0 s | 7.2 to 7.8 s | +484 to +546 MB | 1.54 s | 0.72 s |
| Nebula | 11.1 to 12.8 s | 6.7 to 6.9 s | +458 to +516 MB | 1.65 s | 0.36 s |
| Sparks | 10.4 to 12.9 s | 6.0 to 6.5 s | +460 to +528 MB | 0.67 s | 0.31 s |
| Halo | 9.5 to 11.8 s | 4.8 to 5.0 s | +461 to +527 MB | 0.43 s | 0.20 s |
| Aurora | 10.7 to 13.2 s | 6.2 to 6.8 s | +485 to +546 MB | 0.98 s | 0.29 s |
| Radiance (live engine) | 10.9 to 12.4 s | 6.2 to 6.3 s | +819 to +822 MB | 0.58 s | 0.16 s |

**Cost rows.** `api/_lib/styles/costs.py` `ADDED`, outside the baked block (bake_costs.py leaves it alone), measured on this code and put in the spike's units through the live Radiance measured in the same runs (the method is written beside the rows). At the default slow factor 1.6 a master needs 20.5 to 23.1 s of the 52 s budget and 713 to 722 MB of the 1,434 MB budget: one call, never split. The tiles cost more CPU than release 1's: a one-eye visitor's picker draws eleven tiles instead of six, about 1.9 s more of design per visitor on this machine (Supernova 0.72 s like Powder Burst's 0.65), which fills the per-instance ceiling of 2,000 pictures a day sooner (`TILES_DAY_MAX`, unchanged).

**The build.** Item 15 of `scripts/check_styles.mjs` asks every style the picker shows to have a landing tile; `LANDING_TILE_LATER` names the five, which wait for theirs (the landing is not changed: its gallery is at its byte budget and its slots), only while none of their ceilings is live, and `checkLandingLater` refuses an id of the list that is not in the registry or already has its tile. `scripts/make_style_tiles.py` shows a style of one eye that `SETS` does not name on the owner's own eye (the script writes no style id); the ten tile images of the five are made with it on the owner's eye (a clean rim and an ordinary pupil, the owner's sample rule for every public picture).

**/try.** A tile of one eye prints one line under its name (`picker.lines` in `src/try/copy.ts`, `copy.lt.ts`, `copy.hu.ts`, keyed `line.<id with "_" for ".">` like the reason lines; `src/try/StylePicker.tsx`, one span with classes the tile already has: the stylesheet does not grow). The six of release 1 say what their landing tiles say; the five new lines were written here and need a native read in German, Lithuanian and Hungarian. Seen in a real browser at 375 px against the real engine (`scripts/dev_api.py` and Vite, the AI-generated sample eye, no model call): eleven tiles in the server's order, each with its picture and its line.

**ENGINE_V stays 8.** No picture of an existing style changed: the singles, collision, universe, core and reveal goldens are the old files, byte for byte. The five looks have their own golden file, `scripts/styles_tests/data/lumen_goldens.json` (50 pictures: five looks x three synthetic eye classes at 512 and 1024 px, a 4096 px master of each, the other two canvases and the words; `record_goldens_lumen.py`), which `data/engine_v.json` now lists (v3steps checks the list). A plan made under 8 for an existing style draws the same picture; no order of a new style can predate it. A later change of `nebula.py` or `stardust.py` (by the pairs and families work, say) moves these goldens: record them again with the reviewed boards, then raise ENGINE_V.

**Checks.** The new suite `v3lumen` (89): the family's files and rules, the registry, the costs and the catalogue; the golden replay; the artwork charter on five synthetic eyes (the iris rectangle byte for byte the effect free picture and the graded disc, T1, T2, T3, T6, T7, T12, T18, the black share T4 measured: Nebula 0.05 to 0.13, Supernova 0.46 to 0.57, Halo 0.04 to 0.21, Sparks 0.57 to 0.66, Aurora 0.37 to 0.44), with words and on the other canvases; the seed; the master's plan, capacity and own 4096 px call; `/api/compose` tiles and previews (the engine's pictures with the watermark); the advisory gate on an eye that fails the lid rule (the five stay available, Clean Iris and Celestial Gold are held); the admin laboratory; two and three irises on one canvas; the four languages and the tile images. Pins that named the registry's styles moved with their counts equal, each with its reason in the file: `v3reg` (33 ids, 19 in the laboratory of the world before the cutover, 31 slugs, the policy table, the price loops, the build's line), `v3cutover` (the five at preview, eleven tiles for one eye, fifteen shown ids and their tile images), `v3single` (the customer's list for one eye), `v3checkout` (33 ids). `ts` 754 to 757 (`picker_page.test.ts`: the line on every tile of one eye, the line of a look and none for a style without one, the same eleven keys in four languages).

**Full set**: the private fixtures (`SNAPEYES_FIXTURES`), two suites at a time (the machine is shared), scratch on drive D (`SNAPEYES_SUITES_OUT`): one run on the tree of the first two commits, 45 minutes, **45 of 45 green** (44 suites and `ts`), every count equal to `suites/baseline.json` (`v3lumen` 89 new, `ts` 757, the other 43 as recorded; `oldpay` and `fixer` passed in this run). On the final build, all exit 0: `npm run build` (tsc, `check:prices`, `check:texts`, `check:styles`), `npm run lint` (the warnings of the base, none on the new lines), `check:budget` (landing JavaScript +7,873 B against +7,768 B on `e2754c1`: +105 B, the registry's five entries, which the page imports as text; CSS +7,294 B, unchanged), `check:states` (1,112 of 1,112), `check:gate` (27 of 27) and the Python compile matrix (3.12, 3.13, 3.14: compileall ok, 111 files of api/ parse as 3.12). The third commit, after the run, takes two lines out of the registry's docstring (the text ships in the landing's bundle: 87 B of gzip) and writes the reason of the gate policy into the family's docstring instead; `v3reg`, `v3cutover` and `v3lumen` ran again on it.

**Not measured, risks.** The looks are one reviewer's choice of parameters; the owner has not seen the final Aurora (changed from his laboratory board), nor Nebula's master octaves. Very dark and dark brown eyes look right on the boards (`p05i`, `cm009`), but the owner's sample rule kept `cm010` (a very large pupil) and `cm013` (lid skin) to the internal sheet. The landing's JavaScript now carries the registry's five new entries (the page imports the registry file as text): +7,873 B of the +8,000 B hard stop (+105 B for the five entries; 127 B left), so the pairs and families work cannot add entries without an allowance or without stripping the registry's docstring from the bundle, which would save about 3 kB gzip. A landing tile for each of the five (four sample eyes at 480 and 900 px, about 1 MB of lazy images, the gallery rows, a line in four languages) is about 2 to 3 hours of work and does not fit that budget either. Real phone photos, the gate's verdicts on them and what visitors choose among eleven tiles are not measured.

## 46. The owner's insights on the free preview (2026-10-20): the brown under a thin lid, the words of the watermark, what was done to the photo

Branch `v3-insights` on `v3-int3` e2754c1 (the owner's five points from his own Android photo; point 6, a 4K look link, was cancelled by him and is not here). Three commits, local only: nothing was pushed or deployed. No price, no legal text and no gate rule was touched.

**2. Brown where the lid covered the iris** (`c912996`). Where it came from, measured on the stored chain of 2026-10-12 (wave-l1: client crop, deglared crop, clean preview) with coverage.py's lid cap and the iris at least 60 degrees round from it at the same radius as the reference: on blue, grey and green irises the restoration's cap sat 10 to 16 a*b* units off the eye's own colour (cm013 16.4, cs100 12.6, cm009 12.7, cs024 13.0; the iris itself has a chroma of 1.2 to 3.6), as far as the lid skin in the photo; on a brown iris the same step is 4.5 (cm014), which is why the brown test photos never showed it. `iris.lid_mask` leaves a thin rim lid alone on purpose, so lid_pct was 0 on every one of them (and on the owner's 215208 and his family's d02, d04 and d05, the same blue-grey iris type as his): the lid skin went to the image model as it was and `chroma_lock` kept its colour. Re-locking the stored restorations to their own deglared crops gives their colour back to the digit (the lock is the carrier). Not the lid remnant fill (A21 needs a lid fill), not the void fill, not the colour check (it only measures). Fix in the fill step: `api/_lib/lidcap.py`: when lid_mask finds no lid, the cap analyze already measures (`coverage._lids`, factored out of `lid_cover`, which answers byte for byte as before on 60 squares) becomes a lid mask (the straight cap plus the curved lid pixels touching it, with coverage's own classes and pink skin its block rule does not need) and deglare fills it on the path every found lid takes (mirror_prefill, the lid_drift guard, lid_composite, lid_remnant); the reply says `lid_cap`. `SNAPEYES_LIDCAP=off` switches it off; a crop with no cap is byte for byte as before. The cap of the deglared crop then sits 0.6 to 2.7 a*b* off the iris on the light eyes (cs024 4.8), and re-locking the stored restorations to the new crops gives 1.4 to 5.4 (from 12.5 to 16.4). Six real restorations (about 0.42 USD): the owner's 215208 loses its orange crescent, the family d05 its pink skin and mascara (lid and fill rules then pass), cs100 passes the lid rule it failed; cm013 (one draw) failed the lid rule where its stored draw passed (lashes beyond the cap stay). New suite **v3lidcap** (18, needs the private fixtures: the owner's 215208 and the AI sample's crop). Known limits: a curtain of lashes beyond the cap is not filled (as before); a cap the old detector found takes its old path; a mark drawn on a photo is copied by the donors like any iris pixel.

**4. The words of the watermark** (`f4101be`). The tile is `SnapEyes.com` alone in every language (`iris.WATERMARK_TILE`), laid closer when it is that short (`WATERMARK_STEP`: the long words keep their 0.70 u step and their pixels), so it covers the iris as before (the sample's display copy: 2.3 % of the ring changed by more than 40 levels, 1.6 % at the old step). While ordering is closed (`api/compose.py` `_ordering_soon`: the rule GET /api/checkout tells the page, `pay.ordering_open()` and `store.configured()`, read at most once a minute, closed when unreadable) a free preview of a v3 style carries `WATERMARK_SOON` (FREE PREVIEW · ORDERING OPENS SOON; KOSTENLOSE VORSCHAU · BESTELLUNGEN BALD MÖGLICH; NEMOKAMA PERŽIŪRA · UŽSAKYMUS PRIIMSIME NETRUKUS; INGYENES ELŐNÉZET · A RENDELÉS HAMAROSAN INDUL), narrower than the German open badge on every canvas; open, the badge of before. The legacy engine's previews (retired since the cutover) keep the badge of before. The redraw on a language switch (/api/enhance {redisplay}) works as before; the four languages' copies are one picture now. Moved with a reason: preview B8 and C9 (2 % to 1.5 %: the legacy previews read 1.9 to 2.2 %), v3compose (one check), v3lang (two checks, +6 new: 24), v3reveal and v3reg draw their goldens with the words they were recorded with (the arcs copies; the 22 legacy pixel goldens): those checks are about the arcs and the registry, so no golden file changed and ENGINE_V stays (test_steps.py hashes the golden files); v3reveal +1 check (today's words make another arcs picture, the same in both languages): 73. v3single compares /api/compose with the engine's own picture: its expectation now carries the badge compose draws (the same rule, `_ordering_soon`), +1 check that a closed deployment's preview is not the one with UNLOCK FULL SIZE: 119.

**1, 3, 5. The result screen** (`bce1954`). A card "What we did to your photo" under the before/after of a real eye (`ResultView` `WhatWeDid`): reflections removed (only when one was found; the old line "Reflection removed." and its key go), focus and fibres sharpened, light balanced with the photo's own colours, the part an eyelid covered continued from the eye's own iris (only when deglare filled one: `Eye.lidPct` from its lid_pct), nothing painted on top; the colour lines step aside while the colour note shows, "nothing painted on top" where the Reveal's promise says it; its last line says the photo is the camera's own at full quality, a crop of at most 1400 px is restored, and a 4096 px file would be drawn from the restoration (the 1400 is `src/try/crop.ts` `CROP_MAX_PX`, the very constant of the cut in `TryApp`, passed to the sentence: a number in the copy files that equals a price in Stripe's smallest unit is read as a written price by `check_prices.mjs`, and the exp suite's retired ladder has a step of 1400). The colour note says what helps (even light: daylight from a window, no flash, no torch from the side) and that a result one likes can stay. `suites/ts/what_we_did.test.ts` (35). LT and HU sentences were written without a native reader (Lithuanian for the owner).

**Bytes.** /try first load +1,144 B JS gzip and +9 B CSS against e2754c1 (162,238 to 163,382 B; the base of `check_motion_flow` moves by the JS, none of it motion); the landing does not change (`check:budget` JS +7,715 B, CSS +7,303 B).

**Checks.** On the final build, all exit 0: `npm run build`, `check:texts`, `check:prices`, `check:budget`, `check:states` (1,112), `check:langswitch` (60), `check:polish` (72), `check:tryheader`, `check:motion:flow` (once its "reduced: ring" case failed in a full run and passed twice alone and in the next full run: timing), `oxlint` (no new warning), `compile_matrix.py` (3.12, 3.13, 3.14). The full guard set, the private fixtures, two suites at a time, scratch on drive D: 40 of 45 green on the first pass; the five others were the checks moved above (exp: a number in the copy equal to a step of its retired ladder, now `CROP_MAX_PX`; v3reg, v3reveal: the goldens drawn with the old words; v3single: the badge; v3steps: ENGINE_V, settled by leaving the golden files as they were), and they and the suites of the watermark run green again on the final tree (exp 349, v3steps 152, preview 60, v3compose 126, v3lang 24, v3lidcap 18, v3reg 226, v3reveal 73, v3single 119); ts 789 of 789 (was 754). The old tiles' layer is pixel for pixel the one of e2754c1 at eight sizes (256 to 2048), so the step rule touches the new address only.

## 45. The ensemble family (2026-10-20): the owner's chosen pairs and families of whole irises, at preview

Branch `v3-duofam`, four commits on `v3-singles5` `23a1643` (section 44's light family on `v3-int3` `e2754c1`): the bundle slimming of the registry's text, the five looks, the four cosmos looks, this section. Local only: nothing was pushed or deployed, no Vercel or environment setting was written, no image model was called.

**What the owner chose.** On the laboratory boards of the pairs and families round (`proto_pf`, branch `v3-proto-pf`) he called Clean Duo, Radiance Duo, Family Orbit, Radiance Family and Light Circle "really quite good"; Endless Duo did not please him; the gold looks (Gold Eclipse Duo, Gold Family) stay shelved. Then "taip, darom ... ir poras": he also liked the Nebula and Supernova pair and family sketches of the cosmos laboratory round. His sample rule binds every public picture (tiles, examples): never an eye whose eyelid skin or lashes show above the iris (of the stores we hold cm001, cm009, cm013, cm022, cs044, cs099, cs100, d01, drv_d01 and drv_w03 show them, and cm012, cm016, cm024, cm025 are kept out to be safe), never a set where one pupil is far bigger than the others (cm010, cs003 out; cm014 and cm040 kept out of the public sets); internal test sheets may keep them.

**The family** (`api/_lib/styles/ensemble`, `ENGINE_FAMILIES` gains `ensemble`; named so beside the light family's single look Halo). The laboratory's renderer and pieces ported without a change to what they draw: the plan of a look at the canonical 1024 px, every iris graded and snapped at its size (the singles' `place_iris`, F3 feather, the declared finish at masters), the effect in the float canvas only, tone map, dither, every iris pasted LAST byte for byte, then the customer's names and date by `text.draw_names` (one lockup line, the artwork moved up 0.03 S and shrunk to 0.94, the singles' rule). The contract of the style package: `resolve(spec, profiles, eyes=None)`, `preview`, `tiles`, and `render` for the suites. The seed is `seeds.seed_for_key` of the eyes' ids in canvas order and the plan's seed key (style, design, ground, layout, the canonical options); names, date, canvas, size and pixels are not in it. Options: `swap` for a pair, `rotate` for a group (modulo the eye count; Family Orbit's hub). `checks.selfcheck` runs on every render that asks and on every master: T1, T2, T3, T6, T7, T8, T8b (bounded for the two Radiance looks), T12, T18, T19 and the limb read at 1024 and 320 px (reported).

The looks and what changed from the laboratory:
- **Clean Duo** (`duo.clean_duo`, new): the gold infinity thread the owner saw is the default; `"thread": "tint"` turns each half toward its eye's colour (his other option, shown beside it on the board).
- **Radiance Duo** (`duo.radiance`, was planned): the round he asked for. A dark limb (the graded limb band under 0.34) keeps a wider and deeper moat (up to 0.20 R, the light down to 0.05 at the limb): the laboratory's dark brown set read 1.8 L* at the limb at 320 px, now 9.2. The light fades out from 2.25 R to 2.85 R (`Look.cap` of `singles/radiance.py`: no hero ray across the picture) and over the last 0.035 S toward the frame (`kit.edge_fade`): T8b is bounded and holds on every canvas, the phone wallpaper included (2.2 to 2.4 percent).
- **Family Orbit** (`grp.orbit`, new, layout `orbit`): the first eye of the canvas order is the hub.
- **Light Circle** (`grp.light_circle`, new, layout `ring`): the centre the owner approved (a small white star in a thin aureole); it now moves with the irises when the customer's words make room (the laboratory left it behind).
- **Radiance Family** (`grp.radiance`, was planned, named Radiance Family): the dark limb rule and the edge fade of Radiance Duo; its limb reads 8 to 18 L* on the public sets (the laboratory read 4 to 5.7).
- **Nebula Duo, Nebula Family, Supernova Duo, Supernova Family** (`duo.nebula`, `grp.nebula`, `duo.supernova`, `grp.supernova`, new): `ensemble/cosmos.py` lays the discs out (Radiance Duo's pair with a gap of 0.12 R; Radiance Family's hex rows with a gap of 0.18 D) and calls the light family's effects over all of them, `lumen.nebula.fx_nebula(cv, ctx)` and `lumen.stardust.fx_stardust(cv, ctx)`, the only two entry points it uses; the light family is not changed or copied (`lumen_goldens.json` and `v3lumen` are untouched), so the single looks and these four move together. The nebula's sky reaches the frame (T8b reported: 8 to 14 percent); the burst begins with a white hot rim at every limb, the burst's own light, so Supernova's limb read is low (1.7 to 16 L* on the public sets at a 320 px tile; Nebula Duo reads 2.2 on the owner's blue eye beside the yellow green one, 8 to 33 on the other public pairs; reported, not bounded).
The palette choice of the two Radiance looks (two drawn colours closer than 10 dE00: one eye takes its next variant) depends on the pixels, so it is decided once at the plan with the eyes and frozen (`frozen {"pal": [...]}`, `decided` True; without the eyes `decided` is False and checkout asks for them, as for the collision family); the preview and the master obey it, and `steps._check_drawn` now checks the design and the frozen choices of the ensemble family like the collision and universe families (a master that drew other choices is `picture_drift`, never delivered).

**Registry.** All nine at stage `preview` (drawn in /try for every eye set that passes, never orderable before the owner's tick: `EFFECTIVE_DEFAULT`), the art price class, the HARD gate with the lid rule (Radiance Duo and Radiance Family were planned as advisory), the 2048 px working copy for every eye count, tile orders after the existing pairs and families (Kiss, Collision Infinity, Clean Infinity stay 1 to 3, then Clean Duo, Radiance Duo, Nebula Duo, Supernova Duo; Family Colours 1, Clean Family keeps its reserved 2, then Family Orbit, Light Circle, Radiance Family, Nebula Family, Supernova Family). One new layout word, `orbit` (Orbit, Umlaufbahn, Orbita, Pálya: the Lithuanian and Hungarian want a native read).

**The picker** (`src/try`). On the layout orbit the selected eye has "Put in the centre" (`centreEye` in `TryApp.tsx`: it becomes the first eye, the others keep their order, rotate goes back to 0), pressed and shut on the eye already in the centre, and one line says the first eye is the centre; three eyes also rotate (the server's `rotate`, honoured by every group of the ensemble family). Words in en, de, lt, hu (`picker.options.centre`, `centreLabel`, `centreNote`; `check_texts` has the sample argument); the layout glyph of orbit is the flower's. **The tiles**: `scripts/make_style_tiles.py` reads two more of the photos the owner holds the rights to publish (drv_w06, drv_w09; LICENCIJOS.csv) and only the eyes of the tiles it makes; all nine have their picker tiles (the cosmos looks take the family's default eyes: their slugs carry the word of a legacy id, which no script may write; and the eyes to load now come from `eye_set`: the merge with the light family had left them read from `SETS`, where a style of one eye has no row). The landing's gallery has the five first looks (their pictures, the same 1024 px engine renders as the picker's tiles, their descriptions in four languages, the manifest rows and the provenance); the four cosmos looks wait in `LANDING_TILE_LATER` as the light family does. Every public set obeys the sample rule.

**Bytes.** The landing bundles the registry's text and the layout words (`src/shared/styles.ts`, `layouts.ts` import the Python files with `?raw`), and with the light family its JavaScript stood at +7,873 B of the +8,000 B hard stop. The first commit has the pages read those two files without their docstrings, comments and blank lines and with their literals in compact JSON (`vite.config.ts` `pySourceSlim`, `scripts/py_source_slim.mjs`, which keeps every `NAME = value` line, refuses any other shape and proves on every load that a page reads the same values; `suites/ts/py_source_slim.test.ts`): +3,903 B on `23a1643` (-3,970 B), and +4,905 B on this branch (the five landing tiles and the nine registry entries: +1,002 B). No constant of `check_budget` moved; the hard stop stays. `api/_lib/markets.py` would give 1.7 kB more the same way (not done: the prices are another owner's file).

**Costs** (`api/_lib/styles/costs.py`, a block after the baked one). Every eye count measured in a fresh process the way the master step makes a master (4K eyes at the 2048 px working copy, the preview at 4096 px with the self check, OMP_NUM_THREADS=1, two such processes at a time on a shared machine), the CPU seconds divided by this machine's yardstick 0.951 as MEASURED's rows are. At the default slow factor 1.6 these plans need more than the 52 s work budget (capacity `time`: checkout refuses them once the owner opens the style, never a held order): Family Orbit and Radiance Family at seven and eight eyes (53 to 65 s), Nebula Family from six eyes (58 to 78 s) and Supernova Family from four (58 to 103 s); the pairs need 19 to 40 s (Clean Duo, Radiance Duo, Supernova Duo, Nebula Duo), Light Circle at most 49 s, and every peak stays under 1,000 MB of the 1,434 MB budget. The grade of the eyes at the 2048 px working copy is 13 to 18 s of a master at six to eight eyes, the light of the cosmos effects is drawn over the whole canvas for every iris. Measured beside them the same way, Family Colours at eight eyes takes 26.5 s and 875 MB while its baked capped row says 16.8 s. The 1024 px preview of a cosmos family is slow too: Supernova Family's 1024 px preview of eight eyes takes 16 to 22 s here (the cost table's estimate on the real instance at the slow factor 1.6 is 35 s of the 52 s a call has) and Nebula Family's 8 to 13 s; the preview rows were measured beside a second process and are noisy.

**Tests.** New suite **v3ensemble** (`scripts/styles_tests/test_goldens_ensemble.py`, 46 checks): the files and rules of the family, the registry and the engine table, the golden replay (`data/ensemble_goldens.json`, 67 pictures: sha256 of the decoded pixels on the synthetic eyes of `synth_iris`), the charter and the brief on every golden picture, T9 at 4096 px (Supernova Duo and Family within the brief's bounds; the two Nebula looks held to a floor that names the light family's master octaves, design SSIM 0.88, mean dE00 2.0, p99 11: measured 0.89 and 0.91, reported; without the octaves, the `extra` parameter of `lumen.nebula`, they read 0.95 and 0.96), the contract (resolve with and without the eyes, the frozen palette obeyed, the options, the hub, the words, tiles, the watermark, the guards) and the master plan (every style and eye count, the capacity pinned, a master made the master step's way passing `_check_drawn`). `data/engine_v.json` lists `ensemble_goldens.json`; **ENGINE_V stays 8**: no existing golden changed. The pins that count the styles, the shown ids, the tiles, the landing tiles and the layouts moved on purpose with their counts equal (v3reg, v3cutover, v3checkout, ts); `suites/ts/picker_page.test.ts` checks the hub chooser and its glyph.

**The existing pairs and families did not move.** v3coll 143 of 143 (every golden of Clean Infinity, Collision Infinity, Kiss Collision, the Trio and Family Colours, decoded pixels), v3single, v3uni and v3lumen as recorded; and the pictures themselves through the real engine path, compared by the sha256 of the decoded pixels: Clean Infinity, Collision Infinity and Kiss Collision, the Trio and Family Colours at five and eight eyes, at 1024 px and as 4096 px masters from the 4K restorations at the 2048 px working copy: **14 of 14 identical** between `f3380eb` (the light family's tree with the slimmed bundle) and this branch, and the same 14 identical to the run on `e2754c1` made before the rebase. The singles' `radiance.py` and the light family are untouched (the ensemble family calls them as they are).

**Full set** (the private fixtures, two suites at a time on a shared machine, scratch on drive D:, on the tree of the cosmos commit): **46 of 46 green** (45 suites and `ts`), every count equal to `suites/baseline.json`: v3ensemble 46 new, ts 763 (757, +4 the slimming test, +2 the hub chooser), v3reg 226, v3cutover 79, v3checkout 139, v3lumen 89, v3coll 143, v3uni 142, v3single 118, the rest unchanged. The first two commits were run on their own as well (v3reg 226, v3cutover 79 after the one pin of the catalogue count that the merge had left, v3checkout 139, v3ensemble 45, ts 763). On the final build, all exit 0: `npm run build` (tsc and vite), `check:styles` (40 ids: 6 planned, 4 lab, 19 preview, 5 live, 6 retired; 17 layouts; registry hash `6583f65f95ac`), `check:texts`, `check:prices`, `check:assets` (19 tiles, 13 at preview), `check:states` (1,352 of 1,352), `check:gate` (27 of 27), `check:budget` (JavaScript +4,905 B, CSS +7,294 B over the motionless page, the CSS as before), `check:landing` in real Chrome (ok with the base's two notes: axe-core is not installed, and a currency offer answered after 3 s moves the hero), `python suites/compile_matrix.py` (3.12, 3.13, 3.14).

## 47. The presentation of /try after the owner's own phone (2026-10-20): the before/after leads, the text folds, a calm first screen, the pupil measured

Branch `v3-tryclean` on main `b96c04c` (live). One commit, local only: no push, no deployment, no environment change, no model call. No price, no legal text, no engine rule, no watermark and no gate was touched: `api/` is unchanged.

**What the owner saw** (live /try in Lithuanian, his own green-grey eye, 21:15): the server withheld the Reveal (as for 6 of 7 photos of the QA), so the page showed the strip of three thumbnails and the amber note about a "pjūvis"; under it the card, the transparency sentence, the shared library, the drag line and "Rainelė Jūsų nuotraukoje: 702 px"; "Spalvos paimtos iš Jūsų nuotraukos" twice; the pupil looked huge; the first screen said TIKRAI and carried a lot of text before the photo.

**1. The before/after leads every eye** (`src/try/ResultView.tsx`, `src/reveal/useRevealFrame.ts`). The Reveal when the server gives its numbers and passes them; every other eye, an eye whose Reveal is withheld included, gets the plain slider (`CompareSlider`: the client crop /api/deglare took against the display copy made from it, the same square by construction, so no registration is needed). The withheld strip with its note and the strip of three under a shown Reveal are gone (`src/reveal/RevealStrip.tsx` stays, exported and tested, unused by the page), and no frame is built for a withheld eye any more. The colour note stays under the slider when the colour drifted. No customer sentence names a cut: /try's drag lines and the note went, "Cutting out your iris" is "Cropping your iris", the quality line says "take a close crop" (German "verwenden wir nur den Bereich"), the landing's Reveal intro loses "The cut runs through the pupil:" in four languages and its English alt text and "Look closer" lines say "cropped".

**2. One compact block under the slider.** The colour note (when it drifted), the pupil line (when wide), and one card: "What we did" with at most three check lines (the reflections when found, the focus and fibres, the light; the eyelid line whenever a lid was filled, and the light line steps aside when there would be four) and, under them, ONE disclosure, closed until opened: "How it works" (Kaip tai veikia?, So funktioniert es, Hogyan működik?) with the transparency sentence (decision 5, also the AI disclosure), the promise where the server measured the eye and the colour is fine or else "nothing painted on top" (never both, neither beside the colour note), the sentence of the shared library (word for word: `check_texts` item 12 holds), the camera's quality line and the notes of a small photo. Folded, never deleted. The light line no longer says where the colours come from (the transparency sentence says it once); the iris size in pixels, the frame lines and the drag lines are gone. The disclosure is the browser's own `<details>` with its own triangle: no stylesheet rule of its own (the Tailwind sheet counts in the landing's budget: a chevron that turns and a hidden marker were three rules, 64 B).

**3. The first screen.** "YOUR EYE, AS ART." (JŪSŲ AKIS KAIP MENO KŪRINYS., IHR AUGE ALS KUNSTWERK., A SZEMED MŰALKOTÁSKÉNT.), one sentence under it, the four numbered steps (step 2 and the light line shorter: LIGHT is word for word the retake guide's first step, so the retake cards say the shorter line too), the open eye, the 3 to 5 shots and the pair folded into "More tips" (Daugiau patarimų, Weitere Tipps, További tippek). The photo notice (whose eye may be photographed: legal) keeps its words, one size smaller (10 px).

**4. The pupil, measured on stored data only** (26 eyes: the 22 of the 2026-10-12 chain with client crop, deglared crop and clean restoration, and the four private fixtures; `SP/wave-tryclean/final/pupil`: the table, the sheets, the scatter and the scripts). The pupil's edge (`iris.pupil_circle` edge, else the half-max radius) over the iris radius. The owner's photos: 0.42 (d01), 0.51 (215102), 0.30 (215120), 0.50 (215208) in the photo itself; deglare's vision disc painted none of them (glare on the pupil 0.0 to 0.017; the fill needs 0.04, and `DISC_PUPIL_MAX` keeps the vision disc only where it fills); the restorations are equal (d01 0.41) or smaller (0.44, 0.39). Over the 26 eyes deglare moves the edge by a median of 0.0 (cs044 +0.025 where a window crosses the pupil's edge and the disc paints it; cm016 +0.10 where the photographer's reflection hides the pupil and the disc is the vision estimate). So the pupil is wide in his photos (he shoots indoors in the evening), and the page says one line when the measured pupil is 0.40 of the iris radius or wider (`PUPIL_WIDE` in `src/try/multi.ts`, the engine's own dilated radius; the photo's `quality.pupil_size`, else the Reveal's rho; never for the sample). Not repaired, reported: the image model draws a LARGER pupil than the photo's on 4 of the 22 test photos (cm024 +0.075, cs044 +0.07 of which +0.045 after deglare, cm002 +0.05, cs024 +0.04), and nothing in the chain takes a too large pupil back (`api/_lib/pupil_seal.py` says so). A repair (a radial warp of the render or a second draw) moves the restoration off the photo's registration or needs model spend to validate: for the owner's word.

**Checks moved.** `suites/ts/what_we_did.test.ts` around the new screen (the card's lines per case and at most three, one closed disclosure that holds every long sentence and none of them outside it, the before/after per case, no "cut" in /try's four dictionaries nor in the landing's Reveal, no iris size, the pupil line in eight cases, the first screen's headline, sentence, steps and "More tips"); `suites/ts/reveal_page.test.ts` (the six Reveal keys that remain; a withheld eye shows the plain slider; the withheld colour case is checked by a replacement: the plain slider, the colour note, the retake, no promise, no note). `ts` 792 to 898. `scripts/check_texts.mjs` loses the sample arguments of the removed `result.irisPx`. `check_motion_flow`'s base of /try moves by -1,373 B JS (164,524 to 163,151 B gzip; CSS 13,214 B and /order 111,318 B unchanged): none of it motion.

**Bytes.** `check:budget`: landing JavaScript +7,813 B (b96c04c +7,867 B: the shorter Reveal intros), CSS +7,303 B (unchanged).

**Full set**: the private fixtures, two suites at a time (`SNAPEYES_SUITE_JOBS=2`), scratch on drive D: 48 minutes, **45 of 46 green** on the first pass, every count equal to `suites/baseline.json` (`ts` 898). The one red was `v3wp0`'s rule that no file of suites/ holds an en or em dash: the new test held the range of its own dash check as characters; it is built from the codes now (as `scripts/check_texts.mjs` does), and `v3wp0` (93) and `ts` (898) ran green again on the final tree. In real Chrome on the final build, all exit 0: `check:texts`, `check:budget`, `check:states` (1,112), `check:langswitch` (60), `check:polish` (72), `check:tryheader` (24 pages), `check:landing` (the same 2 notes as b96c04c), `check:motion:flow` (/try +3,063 B JS over its moved base, CSS +1,994 B of +2,048), `check:slots`, `check:reveal`; `npm run lint` 53 warnings as on b96c04c (none on the new lines).

**Screenshots** (`SP/wave-tryclean/final/shots`, before b96c04c and after, 360 x 800 and 412 x 915, Lithuanian and English): the first screen, the AI-generated sample's result, and the result of the owner's own d01 (2026-09-26) with the live server's answer of 2026-10-12 replayed (the Reveal withheld: ok false, drift 16.1; the engine's own `reveal_for` gives the same numbers today) through the local dev API with no model key; the display copies and seals made by the engine's own code with a local secret (`final/tools/make_replay.py`, `shoot.mjs`).

**Not measured, risks.** The Lithuanian and Hungarian (and German) sentences are new and need a native read. The AI note is one tap away now: counsel should say whether a closed disclosure on the same screen is enough. The shorter light line also stands in the retake cards. The owner's photo of tonight is not on this machine; d01 stands in for it.

## 48. Family speed (2026-10-21): the family masters inside a Hobby call at the slow factor 1.6

Branch `v3-famspeed`, two commits on main `b0def00` (section 45's ensemble family, live at preview). Local only: nothing was pushed or deployed, no Vercel or environment setting was written, no image model was called.

**The goal** (the owner: "padaryk didžiųjų šeimų pagreitinimą", make the big families fast; Hobby, no Vercel Pro). The paid 4096 px master of every family look renders inside Hobby's 60 s at the default slow factor 1.6 (here: a fresh process, one thread, at most 35 s with the grade) and checkout's estimate (`costs.assess`, the 52 s work budget) accepts it, so the families can be sold once ordering opens; the previews of eight eyes at about 10 s or less; nothing visible worse at 100 percent.

**Measured first, one process at a time.** `SP\wave-famspeed\tools\hprof.py` makes a master the master step's way in a fresh process with one thread (the eyes decoded from their 4K restorations to the 2048 px working copy, the preview at 4096 px with the self check, the JPEG) and prints where the seconds go; `hprev.py` makes the 1024 px preview three times in a fresh process (cold, warm on new eyes, warm on the same eyes). Other agents kept the machine busy (up to 40 percent of the CPU), so before (`b0def00`) and after were measured alternately, row by row, two to seven samples a row, and the table keeps the fastest sample of a row on both sides. Before, Supernova Family's eight eye master took 41.5 s (37.9 s of render CPU: the bursts 23.9 s, of which the scatter of 336,000 grains about 11 s and the haze on 3 px cells about 8 s; the grade of the eight eyes 10.8 s; the self check 1.9 s), Radiance Family's 28.0 s, Nebula Family's 26.9 s (the gas 9.9 s, the grade 10.4 s). The rows of section 45 were taken two processes at a time and read 20 to 55 percent high for the masters (Supernova Family at eight eyes: 57.6 s in the table's units then, 39.9 s alone) and up to 90 percent for the previews.

**What changed.**
- *Exact, no pixel moves:* `core.splat` adds a channel at a time (numpy's one-dimensional `add.at`, three to five times faster than the add of `(n, 3)` rows, the same additions in the same order on every pixel: every look with particles); `core.ring_at_stack` finds the bins and weights of an angle once for the deep, mid and hot palettes of a ramp (`lumen.common.ramp3`, every grid row of the light family's effects), `lumen.stardust` once for its three angle tables (`_lut_index`, `_lut_at`); the ensemble self check reads the luminance of the four frame strips alone (`checks.frame_luma`, T8b: up to 110 MB less peak). v3ensemble section 7 checks each against the code it replaces, to the bit; every golden replays as recorded.
- *A master's lid map:* `iris.studio_grade(master_map=True)` measures the lid map on the box-averaged copy (`PALE_MAP_MAX`), as every master disc drawn above 1024 px always did; the default is off, so every other caller makes the call it made. The ensemble family's masters (`ensemble.base.MASTER_MAP_FROM`, a canvas of 2048 px and more) ask for it through `core.place_disc`, `core.Iris.graded` (cached apart) and `kit.place_iris`. On the default canvases that reaches the plans whose irises are drawn under 1024 px at 4096, Radiance Family at five, seven and eight eyes, Family Orbit at six to eight and Light Circle at eight (Sd 991 to 1021), which took the preview's exact full-frame map: about 0.3 s an eye. An iris whose eye shows no lid is byte identical; one that reads a lid moves by at most 4 of 255 in its core. T1 stays 0 (the canvas iris is its graded disc; the self check compares with the same grade).
- *The cosmos families' masters from six eyes* (`ensemble.cosmos.GRID_FROM`, `GRID_MASTER`): the light family's effects compute their soft fields on a grid of cells over the whole canvas for every disc (3 px a cell at 4096). A master of six to eight eyes draws Supernova Family's haze on 768 cells asked (5 px a cell, `HAZE_GRID`) and Nebula Family's gas, filaments and dust on the preview's 1024 (4 px, `GAS_GRID`); the stars, grains, sparkles, filaments and young clusters stay full resolution and every particle count stays the laboratory's. Side by side at 100 percent the haze is not told apart; the gas reads slightly softer. The previews, three to five eyes and the pairs are untouched. `lumen.nebula.fx_nebula` and `lumen.stardust.fx_stardust` are called as they are, with their own `grid` option.
- *Tried and not kept:* grading every iris at the size it is drawn (the grade's oversampling 1.0 or 1.25 instead of 1.5, about 0.4 s an eye): the fibres came out softer at 100 percent. Supernova Family from six eyes at the single Supernova's particle density (the counts scaled by the area of its discs, 0.22 at eight eyes): every plan fitted, but the bursts lost most of their glitter, a visible change of the look the owner chose; it stays an option for eight eyes (below). One shared sum of Radiance Family's territories: exact, no faster. An exact scatter add by rounds: slower than `add.at`.

**Result** (`SP\wave-famspeed\final\timing_table.csv` and `.txt`: every look, three to eight eyes, master and preview, the estimate before and after). The 4096 px master, wall seconds here with the eyes' decode, before to after:

| look | 3 eyes | 4 | 5 | 6 | 7 | 8 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Supernova Family | 14.8 to 13.3 | 19.4 to 17.2 | 25.0 to 21.5 | 32.8 to 24.4 | 34.1 to 25.8 | 41.5 to 28.9 |
| Nebula Family | 11.8 to 10.5 | 14.2 to 13.1 | 17.4 to 15.5 | 24.1 to 20.3 | 25.9 to 20.7 | 26.9 to 23.2 |
| Radiance Family | 10.1 to 9.9 | 12.9 to 13.4 | 17.2 to 15.6 | 21.4 to 21.0 | 23.9 to 21.2 | 28.0 to 24.0 |
| Family Orbit | 13.0 to 13.8 | 14.6 to 14.7 | 18.2 to 18.5 | 19.9 to 18.1 | 22.0 to 20.3 | 24.9 to 23.8 |
| Light Circle | 10.0 to 9.8 | 13.9 to 13.7 | 16.3 to 15.5 | 17.7 to 17.1 | 17.5 to 17.9 | 21.5 to 18.4 |

Every master of three to eight eyes takes at most 28.9 s here (the goal's 35 s). At the default slow factor every plan fits one call but Supernova Family at eight eyes (before: twelve refused on time); the slowest that fits is Supernova Family at seven eyes, 48.5 s of the 52 s, then Radiance Family at eight, 46.3 s. The previews of eight eyes (cold, the first of a fresh process): Supernova Family 12.3 to 9.4 s, Nebula Family 7.3 to 6.8 s, the other three 2.9 to 3.6 s as before. A row that moved by about 6 percent or less either way is the machine's noise (Family Orbit at three to five eyes, Light Circle at seven: only the exact refactors reach them). Peaks fall by up to 110 MB; the highest is Radiance Family at eight eyes, 965 MB of the 1,434 MB budget.

**Supernova Family with eight eyes is the one plan still over.** With the laboratory's dense bursts its master takes 25.4 s of render CPU here, 54.0 s of the 52 at 1.6 (the limit is about 24.2 s): checkout answers capacity for it, as before, never a held order. Two ways to sell it, the owner's choice: the single Supernova's particle density at eight eyes only (21.4 s, about 47 s of 52; `SP\wave-famspeed\final\OPTION_supernova8.jpg` shows it beside today's), or keep the look and sell Supernova Family up to seven eyes (`stage_by_eyes`). Nothing else came close: the grade of its eight eyes alone is 11 s.

**Quality** (`SP\wave-famspeed\final\CROPS_*.jpg` and `match_*.json`: the masters of five and eight eyes before and after at 100 percent, the iris that moved most, a meeting point, the effect beyond; SSIM on luma in 7 px windows). Every iris region (r <= R) at SSIM 0.9999 or more. The iris cores (r <= 0.95 R, the charter's zone A) are byte identical in every sheet but the lid map plans, where an eye that reads a lid moves by at most 4 of 255 (Radiance Family two of five and three of eight irises, Family Orbit and Light Circle three of eight). Beyond the irises: Supernova Family of eight SSIM 0.9991 (mean |diff| 0.27 of 255), Nebula Family of eight 0.997 (0.45); every other effect identical. The 4096 px masters of 16 styles this work does not mean to move (the light family's five singles, Radiance, Clean and Universe singles; the four ensemble pairs, Clean and the Universe pair; Family Colours of eight, Infinity Chain of six) are identical by the sha256 of their pixels on both checkouts, and so are 20 previews of the styles with particles at 1024 px.

**ENGINE_V 8 to 9** for the masters above. No golden moved (the goldens are previews; `data/engine_v.json` keeps every hash and names 9); the comment on ENGINE_V now says it also changes when paid masters change that no golden holds.

**Costs** (`api/_lib/styles/costs.py`). The MASTER, CAPPED and PREVIEW rows of the five groups from the after side of the table (the render's CPU divided by the yardstick 0.951, the peak the high-water mark); the pairs' rows stand. `DECODE_S_PER_EYE` says 0.25 s an eye; the 4K restorations took 0.36 s here (with that, Supernova Family at seven eyes would need about 50 s of the 52). Still refused, untouched: Family Colours with seven eyes on memory (1,447 MB of the 1,434 MB from its baked row, as v3coll pins it, outside release 1; it peaked at 869 MB here, and a capped row of its own would let it through) and the laboratory's Universe of three eyes (1,438 MB; peaked at 1,097 MB). Infinity Chain (laboratory, three to six eyes) takes 10 to 14 s.

**Tests.** v3ensemble 46 to 50: section 7 checks the master's lid map against the log of every picture of the replay, the options the cosmos families hand the light family for each eye count and size, and the three exact refactors to the bit (the old `splat` kept in the test as the reference); the capacity pin is `OVER = ["grp.supernova/8"]`.

**Checks.** The full set with the private fixtures, two suites at a time, scratch on drive D: 41 minutes, **47 of 47 green** on the final tree, every count equal to `suites/baseline.json` (v3ensemble 50); `v3wp0` (93) once more after this section was written (its first run lost a local socket in the cpu_probe redirect test, WinError 10053, and stopped at 74; the second ran clean). On the final tree, all exit 0: `check:styles`, `check:states` (1,352 in real Chrome), `check:gate` (27 of 27 cases), `check:budget` (JavaScript +4,830 B, CSS +7,303 B; no page file changed), `check:texts` and `suites/compile_matrix.py` (Python 3.12, 3.13, 3.14). No page changed, so `check:motion:close` was not run.

## 49. The calm flow (2026-10-22): a usable photo goes straight on, no broken tile, a drag without a detent, no tagline, the price list

Branch `v3-calm`, one commit on main `2638331` (live). Local only: nothing was pushed or deployed, no Vercel or environment setting was written, no image model was called. `api/` is unchanged.

**What the owner saw** (his own Android phone on the live site, 2026-10-22): a photo taken under a lamp that "is really not bad" stopped on a screen that said SILPNA NUOTRAUKA, "Detalumas 25/100, siekite 70+" and two tips ("everywhere I tried: it does not fit and does not fit"; "we exist to RAISE the iris quality, not to tell every customer what does not fit"); the before/after vibrated and snapped in the middle while he dragged it; the Universe tile showed a warning sign and "Pirmiausia nufotografuokite akį 1 iš naujo"; the header of /try said "PRIVATE ATELIER · PERŽIŪRA"; every row of the landing's price list said NETRUKUS.

**1. The quality step decides differently** (`src/try/shots.ts`, `src/try/TryApp.tsx`). `autoContinue` was `usable && verdict === 'good' && !lamp_cast`: of the 25 usable real photos of the stored chain (SP wave-l1, the live server's answers of 2026-10-12) 19 read weak and 6 ok, none good, so EVERY one of them stopped on the quality screen. It is `usable` now: a photo the studio can restore goes straight to the restoration whatever its verdict, Detail or lamp colour; what stops is only what the engine cannot restore and gives no work ticket (a circle that did not lock onto the iris edge, a shot blocked as too blurry, too dark, a pupil or an eyelid that leaves too little iris, an iris too small). The single-photo screen shows the preview, the reason in one line and, for a blocked photo, the retake steps for that reason; a usable photo is on it only when its restoration failed (the error says why) and its button is "Try again". The shot collector lost its verdict pill, its Detail meter, the Detail in its header and under its thumbnails (a blocked shot's thumbnail keeps its one word). The lamp gets ONE calm line while the iris is restored (`calmNote`: the server's own lamp sentence in the page's language, else the pupil note, else none), never a screen. The numbers stay where they were: the ranking of several shots reads verdict and Detail, the server logs them (`snapeyes capture`, the admin's events), `meta` still carries the quality. Removed with the screens: `meterBand`, `shownDetail`, `fibreRatio`, the components `Verdict`, `DetailMeter`, `ShotNotes`, and their words in four languages (`quality.verdicts`, `detail`, `aimFor`, `fibresResolved`, `picked`, `pickedRatio`, `continueAnyway`, `tryRetake`, the `badge` of every block reason, `collector.headerDetail`; `scripts/check_texts.mjs` loses their sample arguments).

**2. The drag of the Reveal** (`src/reveal/Reveal.tsx`, `revealMath.ts`). No `navigator.vibrate` and no snap to the pupil cut within 3 percent: the cut follows the finger (`dragCut`, a clamp). The arrival sweep, the keys (Enter still goes to the pupil cut) and the double tap (whose tolerance is still `SNAP`) are unchanged; the landing's slider never snapped.

**3. The header of /try** holds the logo and the language switch, nothing else (`header.tag` removed in four languages; the family test's badge only with ?study=1). `check:tryheader` now also fails a header that carries more than those two (the build of 2638331 fails it on all 24 pages).

**4. A held style is not a tile** (`src/try/StylePicker.tsx`, `ResultView.tsx`, `BuyCard.tsx`). A tile the gate, a pupil shape or an unmeasured preview holds back for these eyes is left out of the grid (`heldTile` in `picker.ts`); a list with nothing left to show draws no empty grid; whether a group "opens soon" is still read from every tile of the list. The amber retake card under the list (title, reasons, tip, "greyed out", the e-mail) is gone. Where no style at all can be drawn, one calm line and the retake button stand where the picture would be (`CalmRetake`: "Kad parodytume stilius, reikia dar vienos nuotraukos."; the remove button stays for a pupil the styles cannot take in an artwork of several eyes). The eye chips are marked only for an eye the style on screen warns about, or when nothing can be drawn. The advisory line under the artwork is one calm line (no warning sign). The buy card of "every style that can be bought is held back" keeps its line without "(see above)" and carries the retake button itself. Unused now: the words of the card and of the held tile (`picker.retakeFirst`, `resealFirst`, `pupil`, `retake.title`, `lid`, `reflection`, `reseal`, `held`, `tips`, `noPreview`, `retake.pupil`), removed in four languages. `picker.ts` keeps its state functions as they were (`retakeView`, `buyState`, `tileState` and the rest) and gains two pure helpers, `heldTile` and `retakeEyes`.

**The Universe finding** (`SP/wave-calm/final/tools/fill_stats.py`, the numbers in `SP/wave-calm/final/universe/fill_stats.json` and `.log`; the engine's own `profile_of_bytes` on the stored clean restorations, local CPU only, no rule changed). His photo of today is not on this machine and the production logs are not available, so its numbers are not known; what the tile says is: "retake eye 1 first" (the gate kind, not "make the preview again"), the other tiles drawn, so his restoration passed the lid rule and failed Universe's fill rule. On the 25 real restorations (22 of the 2026-10-12 chain, whose live seals the local measure matches on all 22, and the owner's three recorded fixtures) the fill rule holds Universe on 9 (36 percent): fill_lid_margin (run18 >= 12 with n_step >= 55) on 7, fill_rim_sector (run18 >= 60) on 4, fill_hard_edges (n_step >= 120) on 1 and never alone (cm014, 137, with the lid margin), catchlight on 0; on 6 of the 25 (24 percent) only Universe is held while the lid styles are drawn. n_step over the 25: median 68, 25 to 137. The owner's own four restorations pass it (n_step 68, 72, 80, 105). fill_hard_edges alone held the AI-generated sample before its re-bake (n_step 127). So the hard edges arm is rare on real photos; the long run of off-colour rim sectors (a lid margin or a rim sector, run18) is what holds Universe back.

**5. The landing's price list** (`src/landing/PriceTable.tsx`, `src/landing/copy/*.json`). While the page says that ordering opens soon (the notice's own flag) the table is the shop's price list: every row prints the server's price in the market's currency (Clean Iris 19,97 €, an art style 24,97 €, two eyes 39,97 €, three eyes 54,97 € with "two eyes cost 39,97 €, each further eye +15,00 €, up to 8 eyes"), no row says Soon or names a style, and the notice above is the ONE soon line (it lost its second sentence: "Užsakymus pradėsime priimti netrukus.").
